[[smc-vplan]]
== SMC OCAH Open-Source Verification Plan

=== Overview

This plan covers the SMC subsystem as it is verified in the open-source
harness: the `smc_wrapper` DUT driven over SEP_IN AXI and AXI-Lite, with
cocotb/PyUVM as the primary framework and an SV-UVM view for the scenarios
that carry one. No run in this package executes the SV-UVM view: every
`result.json` under `build/runs/` records `framework: cocotb`. The checker
rows marked `SV-UVM shape only` therefore describe that view and close
nothing for this plan's sign-off; a card's cocotb checkers alone carry its
Pass Criteria here, and a card whose checkers were all SV-UVM would be
unclosed. The enrolled testcases and the membership of the `all`
sign-off group are defined in `testlists/all.toml`; `all` is every test this
plan grades, partitioned into the `hosted`, `fw` and `dual` classes by what a
leaf needs to build. Every defined leaf outside `all` is in
`testlists/holdout.toml` and has its card under <<smc-held-out-testcases>>;
where the reason is a defect, Known Limitations records the hole.

Every card under Test Procedures names a testcase that exists and is a member
of `all`, and says which groups enrol it. Where the harness
cannot reach a feature, the testcase is not listed here as enrolled; it is
recorded under Known Limitations with the reason, so this plan states what
is verified rather than what was once intended.

=== Verification Strategy

* *Frontdoor stimulus, frontdoor proof.* Stimulus is driven through product
  ports -- SEP_IN AXI, AXI-Lite, JTAG, and the pad-level protocol VIPs -- and
  every claim is proven by what the DUT returns or drives at those ports and
  on its own outputs. Where the DUT offers no frontdoor way to create the
  condition a testcase verifies -- a corrupted ECC codeword in a scratch
  macro, an unused encoding in a state flop, an error from the eFuse bank
  model -- the testcase may create it with a testbench hook: a backdoor write
  into the macro array, a force on the flop, or an inject pin on the bound DV
  memory collateral. Such a card names the hook on its *BFM / Model* line and
  in its Test Procedure, and the DUT's reaction is still observed frontdoor,
  on the DUT's outputs and registers, never on the poked or forced signal
  itself. Passive hierarchical taps read state and never write it. The rule
  holds for the `all` sign-off sweep and for every card that claims sign-off
  PASS; a leaf whose checks still read the forced signal is held out of `all`
  and recorded under Known Limitations until its checks move to the registers.
* *Checked, not observed.* A read that is printed but not compared is not
  evidence. Expectations come from the generated RDL reset values, the
  preloaded assets, or the specification -- never from a value the DUT
  returned earlier in the same run.
* *Negative checks carry a positive control.* A claim that a signal stays
  low is only meaningful if the same run shows it going high.
* *Evidence tokens.* Each testcase emits `CHK-*` lines that name what was
  compared and against what, so a kept log can be read without the source.

=== Naming Rules

* Testcases are `smc_<feature>_<scenario>_test`, lower snake case.
* The cocotb module and the testlist entry share that name.
* Sequences are `<testcase>_seq`, in `cocotb/seq_lib/`.
* Evidence tokens are `CHK-<AREA>-<CLAIM>`, upper kebab case.

=== BFM Policy

Shared and vendored components are used where they exist. A local model is
written only where none fits, and every one carries a README beside it
naming what it replaces and why -- see `dv/models/README.md`.

Bus terminators answer a port that a real integration fills with the
adopter's CSRs. They are declared as terminators, never presented as
verifying the block behind the port.

=== Verification Scope

This plan claims SMC behaviour at the `smc_wrapper` boundary and over the
SEP_IN AXI and JTAG-AXI ports, with the DUT elaborated at the open-source
integration's parameter defaults (four CPU cores, 256 external interrupts,
32 mailbox pairs, the 1 KiB eFuse map). The behaviours below sit behind
that boundary or belong to another level's plan; a green SMC regression says
nothing about them, and Known Limitations records the disposition of each.

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Behaviour |Why it is out of scope here
|The adopter's PLL and PVT blocks |Placeholder RTL in this harness; there is no design behind the register interface
|Everything behind the mandatory external AXI-Lite window (per-pad controls, clock-observation and pad-bias blocks) |Adopter implementation; this plan proves only the decode into the window
|The DTP CSR window and the raw OTP window |No responder on `smc_wrapper`; the boundary is left idle rather than answered by a placeholder
|The cluster PLIC register file from the external port |The aperture accepts no writes from SEP_IN; PLIC programming is proven only by the firmware leaves that run on the CPU
|I3C CCC and IBI scenarios needing device-address-table state |No DAT/DCT RAM in `tb_top`; the I3C leaf is decode-only
|The eFuse macro and the SEP lifecycle policy behind `lc_state_i` |An `efuse_bank_model` answers the sense; the lifecycle policy is SEP-level verification and the bench drives the resolved state
|The SMU integration of the SMC (`smu_wrapper` straps, cool-reset pin, boundary I/O) |The SMU plan (`hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_VPLAN.adoc`)
|===

[[smc-scope-traceability-candidate]]
=== Scope and Traceability

This section is the requirement-to-test-to-coverage matrix. Live group
membership is `hw/sys/smc/dv/testlists/all.toml` and the lists it includes;
`hw/sys/smc/dv/docs/SMC_VPLAN.adoc` states what each testcase verifies. This
file is the reconciliation between the two — which of those testcases count as
feature credit, and which are bring-up or decode-only names that must not be
counted twice.

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Field |Value
|DUT |`--dut smc` → `smc_wrapper` under `smc_uvm_top`
|Spec / plan |`hw/sys/smc/dv/docs/SMC_VPLAN.adoc` plus the groups
`hw/sys/smc/dv/testlists/all.toml` resolves
|Coverage intent |`hw/sys/smc/dv/docs/SMC_FCOV.adoc` (Python counters; no
Verilator covergroup)
|Checker |`SmcScoreboard` non-vacuous SAMPLE/AXI/VIP counts + `results.xml`
|CI |`.github/workflows/sim.yml` PR `smoke`; `.github/workflows/regress.yml` nightly and weekly `hosted`
|===

[[smc-live-groups]]
==== Live groups versus credit

Executable membership is resolved from the testlists; the groups and their
`expected_count` guards are in `hw/sys/smc/dv/testlists/all.toml`:

[cols="2,3",options="header",]
|===
|Group |Role
|`smoke` |PR gate (`sim.yml`): canonical bring-up, cold reset, and a CSR frontdoor read
|`hosted` |Nightly and weekly (`regress.yml`): `all` minus the seventeen leaves that need the RISC-V toolchain (fourteen `fw` leaves, three dual-target leaves that run a ROM or firmware image)
|`rtl_issue` |Weekly on its own `regress.yml` row: defect reproducers graded under `expect_fail`; no feature credit
|`all` |The package regression, `hosted` + `fw` + `dual`; `holdout.toml` defines the leaves outside it and <<smc-held-out-testcases>> records each
|===

`smoke` is the three-leaf PR gate (`smc_canonical_smoke_test`,
`smc_cold_reset_test`, `smc_register_sanity_test`). `smc_i3c_to_fabric_test`
is in `hosted` but not `smoke`, and receives no feature credit.

[[smc-credit-rules]]
==== Credit rules

* Feature credit requires active CSR/fabric/pin stimulus and a
  non-vacuous `SmcScoreboard` count. SAMPLE-only bring-up stays in
  `hosted` (`smc_cold_reset_test` is also in `smoke`) and is not a
  requirement row.
* `smc_i3c_to_fabric_test` is HCI_VERSION decode (`proxy=True`). It
  remains runnable by name. It is not
  stub-signature SLVERR, and it is not I3C protocol credit.
* Names in <<smc-deferred-disposition>>, adopter PLL/PVT OKAY+0 wraps, and
  TB-glue DFD tokens are OUT.
* UART loopback / ROM firmware stay on their existing intake issues.
  They are not re-scoped here.
* `--dut smc` already instantiates `smc_wrapper`. There is no separate
  `wrapper.toml` and no test named `main_wrapper` or `main_wrapper_por`.
  Bring-up / POR successors are `smc_canonical_smoke_test` and the reset
  cluster (`smc_cold_reset_test`, `smc_powergood_glitch_test`,
  `smc_multi_reset_csr_persistence_test`).

[[smc-traceability-matrix]]
==== Matrix (feature-credit subset)

Requirement IDs are the VPLAN TC rows or the canonical scenario those
tests actually run. Coverage bins are the `hw/sys/smc/dv/docs/SMC_FCOV.adoc`
families the scoreboard can hit; Verilator does not close SV covergroups.

The Groups column is resolved membership, not intent. Every feature-credit row
below is in `all` and `hosted`, and every one but `smc_cpu_to_sep_axi_test` is
the column names the feature groups that also carry it, so a
reviewer can run one feature without the whole regression.

[cols="2,3,2,2,3,1",options="header",]
|===
|Requirement |Test |Feature groups besides `all` and its class |Coverage intent |Checker evidence |Credit
|Reset / power-good bring-up |`smc_canonical_smoke_test` |combined, smoke |reset + combined SAMPLE |Scoreboard SAMPLE counts; `results.xml` |YES
|SMC misc / scratch / chip-config CSR |`smc_register_sanity_test` |smoke |axil_csr |AXI readback vs expected; non-vacuous AXI |YES
|Default / RO register read |`smc_default_reg_rd_test` |-- |axil_csr |AXI RO data match |YES
|Mailbox STATUS / IRQ |`smc_mailbox_irq_test` |-- |mailbox |IRQ observe + mailbox CSR |YES
|Local fabric CSR wr/rd |`smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test` |-- |fabric |AXI wr/rd on input+output windows |YES
|Function-level reset |`smc_flr_sanity_test` |-- |reset |FLR then CSR recover |YES
|I2C master/target active |`smc_i2c_master_target_test` |i2c |i2c (active, not SAMPLE-only) |I2C agent + scoreboard |YES
|iJTAG / TAP-adjacent |`smc_ijtag_basic_test` |-- |jtag |JTAG VIP / CSR precheck |YES
|CPU-control / SEP_IN path |`smc_cpu_to_sep_axi_test` |-- |cpu_traffic |AXI to CPU_CTRL window |YES
|eFuse / OTP clock observe |`smc_efuse_otp_clock_test` |-- |efuse_otp |Clock/CSR observe (not burn) |YES
|GPIO IRQ active |`smc_gpio_irq_active_test` |gpio |gpio + interrupt |Active GPIO IRQ, not SAMPLE-only |YES
|AVSBus / sideband smoke |`smc_sideband_protocol_smoke_test` |-- |avsbus |Sideband CSR/status |YES
|UART/SPI/log-engine CSR |`smc_uart_spi_log_engine_test` |-- |uart_spi (CSR; not loopback) |CSR window; protocol loopback is a P2 intake row |YES
|Zeroer payload clear |`smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test` |-- |zeroer datapath |Output-fabric responder readback of the zeroed region; CTRL_STATUS follows the zeroer (no timeout path is exercised) |YES
|Outbound filter / remap |`smc_output_filter_remap_security_test` |-- |fabric filter |AXI filter/remap |YES
|ECC / DFD / DBS sanity |`smc_ecc_dfd_dbs_sanity_test` |-- |dbs_dfd_ecam_ecc |CSR sanity; fault-inject is deferred |YES
|Multi-reset CSR persist |`smc_multi_reset_csr_persistence_test` |-- |reset |Scratch persist across reset classes |YES
|I3C window decode |`smc_i3c_to_fabric_test` |-- |i3c decode |OKAY + `HCI_VERSION=0x120`; `proxy=True` |NO
|PLL/PVT clock config |none; `smc_pll_pvt_clock_config_test` is dispositioned in <<smc-deferred-disposition>> |none |macro handshake |OKAY+0 wrap |NO
|I3C CCC/IBI |none; `smc_i3c_ccc_ibi_full_test` is dispositioned in <<smc-deferred-disposition>> |none |— |child I3C TB |NO
|DTP CSR on smc_wrapper |idle port; no terminator |none — no terminator |— |do not add a placeholder slave for credit |NO
|UART/SPI loopback, ROM boot |P2 / firmware groups |not canonical |protocol / firmware |UART/ROM intake |NO
|===

The SAMPLE-only observe names are (`smc_cold_reset_test`,
`smc_clk_running_test`, `smc_gpio_observe_test`, `smc_i2c_cg_sanity_test`,
`smc_irq_observe_test`, `smc_mailbox_idle_test`,
`smc_input_fabric_axi_wr_rd_test`, `smc_avsbus_sanity_test`,
`smc_dbs_idle_test`). Those stay category aliases. They are not extra
feature rows.

|`smc_fw_uart_log_engine_boundary_backpressure_test` |Log-engine effective-length boundaries -- request shorter than the slot, request clamped to the slot, non-word-aligned slot rounded to one beat -- and a log write under UART TX backpressure.
|`smc_fw_uart_log_engine_disable_during_xfer_test` |CTRL.EN cleared mid-transfer halts the engine, measured as at least one but fewer bytes than the entry reaching the UART and none after it goes idle; a re-trigger delivers exactly its bytes; four entries triggered together all hwclr; the same halt measurement on an abort from WAIT; replica 1 runs an entry.
|`smc_fw_uart_log_engine_fetch_err_test` |A log-engine fetch from an unmapped address latches INTR_STATUS.LOG_FETCH_ERR: INTR_TEST self-test, INTR_ENABLE gating, LOG_FETCH_ERR as an event (with the engine stopped a W1C holds) and a good fetch then running from a cleared status, sustained DECERR with LOG_CTRL not hwclr, WRITE_ERR staying clear, and the fault on replica 1.

=== Test Procedures

==== MISC

[[sec-001-smc_5agent_observability_test]]
===== 1 `smc_5agent_observability_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_5agent_observability_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: SMC OSS PyUVM 5-agent full observability test.

*Test Coverage*:

* A bare idle IRQ sample, whose `sync_irq` / `gpio_irq_any` / `uart_irq_any` == 0 compares also pass on a stuck-at-0 or mis-bound probe, proves nothing on its own. The SAMPLE is therefore preceded by a **positive control**: GPIO0 is armed over the real SEP_IN AXI frontdoor and driven low at the pad until `tb_gpio_irq_any` is observed at 1, and an IRQ item carrying `expect_gpio_irq_any = 1` is sampled inside that window (`[NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]`).
* A single SAMPLE gated only on `item.resolvable` gives `CHK-5AGENT-GPIO` no FAIL-ON path, because `resolvable` cannot be False under the 2-state simulator every retained run uses. The GPIO leg is therefore a *pair* of SAMPLEs whose expectation is stated on the **raw pad-bus vectors** (`tb_core2pad_o` / `tb_core2pad_en_o`), backed in the same run by `ensure_gpio_pad_bus_control`. The three `tb_gpio_*_any` aggregates are unbackable in this TB and stay OBSERVED-ONLY -- the scoreboard refuses a stated expectation on them (`[EVIDENCE-TOKEN-CONDITIONAL]`, `[NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]`).
* The 5-agent composition claim is gated on the **per-type** scoreboard counters, not on the aggregate `total > 0` gate, so a missing item type is a composition failure instead of an invisible hole (`[EXACT-EXPECTATION]`).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-5AGENT-CLK` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-COMPOSITION` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-GPIO` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-I2C` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-IRQ-IDLE` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-IRQ-POSITIVE` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-RESET` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-5AGENT-CLK`, `CHK-5AGENT-COMPOSITION`, `CHK-5AGENT-GPIO`, `CHK-5AGENT-I2C`, `CHK-5AGENT-IRQ-IDLE`, `CHK-5AGENT-IRQ-POSITIVE` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `combined`.
'''''

[[sec-002-smc_6agent_observability_test]]
===== 2 `smc_6agent_observability_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_6agent_observability_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI-Lite)

*Objective*: SMC OSS PyUVM 6-agent full observability test.

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequence for smc_6agent_observability_test.
* All six agents (reset / i2c / clk / irq / gpio / axil) sampled in one scenario.
* The ultimate full-env demonstration.
* Each agent's mapped expectation is asserted here and only then does the body emit that agent's `CHK-6AGENT-<AGENT>:` evidence token, so no token can be printed from the setup path or from an item whose compare did not run (`[EVIDENCE-TOKEN-CONDITIONAL]`).
* The composition claim is gated on the **scoreboard's typed counters** (`EXPECTED_SAMPLES`), which are incremented on the analysis path: a mis-bound agent, a dropped item type or a monitor that never published fails the gate.
* The ordered `chk_seen` list is kept only as a readable record of which legs ran -- it is appended by `_chk` itself in the same straight-line body, so comparing it against a literal could not fail on any RTL, so it is not the composition check (`[NO-ALWAYS-PASS-CHECKER]`).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-6AGENT-AXIL` |AXIL compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-CLK` |CLK compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-COMPOSITION` |Named compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-6AGENT-GPIO` |GPIO compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-I2C` |I2C compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-IRQ` |IRQ compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-RESET` |RESET compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-6AGENT-AXIL`, `CHK-6AGENT-CLK`, `CHK-6AGENT-COMPOSITION`, `CHK-6AGENT-GPIO`, `CHK-6AGENT-I2C`, `CHK-6AGENT-IRQ` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `combined`.
'''''

[[sec-003-smc_canonical_smoke_test]]
===== 3 `smc_canonical_smoke_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_canonical_smoke_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI-Lite)

*Objective*: Run six-agent observability across the reset recovery matrix.

*Test Coverage*:

* Canonical high-density SMC smoke sequence.
* This sequence combines the six-agent observability smoke with the reset recovery matrix in one canonical test.
* Synchronization contract: no step waits a magic number of `clk_ref_i` cycles and then samples.
* Every one is a bounded poll on the real reset observables via `SmcResetOp.WAIT_STATE` -- the driver polls until the exact expected state appears and the scoreboard converts bound expiry into a testcase failure with the last observed state (`[NO-BLIND-DELAY-SYNC]`, `[TIMEOUT-MUST-FAIL]`).
* Mid-window `RAW_SAMPLE` snapshots carry the exact asserted expectation they are taken for, so they are checked evidence instead of observations (`[EXACT-EXPECTATION]`).
* The remaining fixed `ClockCycles` are *driven stimulus widths* (how long a pin is held low), which is the quantity the sequence owns, never a stand-in for a completion handshake.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CANONICAL-SMOKE` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-COLD_PRIMARY_ASSERTED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-COLD_RELEASED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-COOL_PRIMARY_ASSERTED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-COOL_RELEASED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-PG_ASSERTED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-PG_RELEASED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-CANONICAL-SMOKE`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-COLD_PRIMARY_ASSERTED`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-COLD_RELEASED`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-COOL_PRIMARY_ASSERTED`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-COOL_RELEASED`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-PG_ASSERTED` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `combined`, `smoke`.
'''''

*Objective*: SMC OSS PyUVM combined-observability test.

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequence for smc_combined_observability_test.
* Cross-agent sequence: samples both the reset observables and the I2C observables from the same scenario.
* Uses dual-sequencer dispatch via the respective agent sequencers, demonstrating the multi-agent env pattern end-to-end within a single test scenario.
* Dispatching the two items and returning would leave the retained evidence as scoreboard prose only, with nothing asserted locally.
* The body therefore restates both expectations -- the five reset observables at 1 (released) and `tb_i2c_cg_en == 0`, whose same-run positive control is the test's declared `probe_positive_controls = ("i2c_cg_en",)` -- reconciles both dispatched items against the scoreboard's typed counters, and only then emits one `CHK-COMBINED-*` token per leg (`[EVIDENCE-TOKEN-CONDITIONAL]`).

'''''

[[sec-005-smc_default_reg_rd_test]]
===== 5 `smc_default_reg_rd_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_default_reg_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Run a compact OSS-safe default-register read sweep.

*Test Coverage*:

* Default register read smoke over the real SEP_IN AXI ingress port.
* The sweep reads side-effect-free internal SMC CSRs through `sep_axi_in_req_i` and checks that every selected address returns an OKAY AXI response **and** — wherever the generated register map defines one — the exact reset value for that register.
* Access-port identity: the test starts this sequence on `env.sys_axi_agent`, whose driver declares `bus_prefix = "s_axi"` / `bus_name = "SEP_IN AXI"` (`env/smc_sys_axi_agent.py`), and `tb_top.sv` wires the top-level `s_axi_*` pins into `smc.sep_axi_in_req_i`.
* The SYS_IN port (`sys_axi_*` -> `sys_axi_in_req_i`) is driven by the separate `env.sys_in_axi_agent`, which this test never starts; despite its name, `env.sys_axi_agent` drives SEP_IN.
* What that does and does not prove: every register read below lives inside `smc_misc_wrap` and is reached over the same internal register fabric from either ingress port, so the reset-default proof holds regardless of which port carried it.
* It gives NO coverage of the SYS_IN ingress port itself.
* Addresses come from the generated PeakRDL map only (indexed `SCRATCH_BASE_ADDR(idx)` macros and per-register `*_BASE_ADDR` symbols in `hw/sys/smc/regs/gen/c/smc_addr.h`); expected values come from `smc_csr_field_catalog` so the address and the value share one source.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_default_reg_rd_test_seq`. The scenario has two implementations that share this
claim: the cocotb `smc_default_reg_rd_test_seq.py` and the SV-UVM
`smc_default_reg_rd_test_seq.svh` (`--framework uvm`). Each checker below is a named compare
the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds. The SV-UVM view reads only
-- it writes nothing -- which is what lets it claim the registers still hold their reset value.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DEFAULT-REG-SWEEP` |Named compare in `smc_default_reg_rd_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-DEFAULT-REG-VALUE` |Named compare in `smc_default_reg_rd_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-DEFAULT-REG-VALUE-COMPARE-FLOOR` |Named compare in `smc_default_reg_rd_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-REG-DEFAULT` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): each catalogued register reads back its generated `*_REG_DEFAULT`. Two expectations are non-zero (`CHIP_CONFIG.VERSION_LO` = 0x000100A0, `RESET_UNIT.SS_WARM_RESET_N` = 0xFFFFFFFF), so a read path stuck at zero cannot satisfy the catalogue. One compared entry, `NDM_RESET.NDMRESET_REQUEST`, is `sw=r; hw=w` and its value arrives from the harness tie-off `tb_ndmreset_request = '0` at a DUT primary input -- the specified source for such a field, so the register block's decode and read path are in the loop, but the value is not register storage
|(decode-only entries) |SV-UVM: registers whose read value this bench does not establish carry no checker of their own -- the OKAY claim on the access is `CHK-CSR-RESP`, and the observed word is logged as an `OBSERVED-ONLY` observation rather than a check. `CHIP_CONFIG.LC_STATE` is driven by the harness, `NDM_RESET.NDMRESET_CLUSTER_COUNT` by a design-side count; both read something other than their RDL default, so comparing against it would be an invented claim
|`CHK-CSR-RESP` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): every SEP_IN CSR access completes with an OKAY response
|`CHK-FUSE-SENSE-DONE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): warm-reset domain release after fuse sense completes
|`CHK-NONVAC` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): reconciles the accesses the body issued against its own loop bounds -- a control-flow invariant that catches a truncated pass, not a DUT-failable compare. `CHK-SB-MIN-ACTIVITY` is the observed-side counterpart
|`CHK-SB-MIN-ACTIVITY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): the `default_reg` feature's comparison count, taken from the always-on scoreboard fed by the PASSIVE monitor, is at or above the floor the completed passes imply (one predicted read per catalogue entry carrying a default)
|`CHK-SB-DEFAULT-REG` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): always-on scoreboard, feature `default_reg`: `smc_default_reg_ref_model` predicts every catalogued read from the generated defaults plus the writes it observed. A required feature with zero comparisons fails the run
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-DEFAULT-REG-SWEEP`, `CHK-DEFAULT-REG-VALUE`, `CHK-DEFAULT-REG-VALUE-COMPARE-FLOOR` hold after their compares. Under `--framework uvm` -- a framework no run in this package executes, so the SV-UVM rows above are not closed by any run here -- the governing checker set is the SV-UVM one in the table above -- the cocotb ids named at the head of this Pass Criteria do not run there -- and it holds instead, and `+SMC_DEFAULT_REG_SCOREBOARD_NEGATIVE` must fail the run. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-007-smc_fw_plic_claim_test]]
===== 7 `smc_fw_plic_claim_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_fw_plic_claim_test`

*BFM / Model*: the SMC C image `fw/tests/plic_sanity`, executed by the CPU cluster; stimulus on `tb_ext_interrupt_0_i`, verdict read over SEP_IN AXI

*Objective*: Firmware programs the cluster PLIC and claims a real external interrupt through it.

*Test Coverage*:

* The cluster PLIC's enable, priority, threshold and claim-complete path, which **no cocotb sequence in this package can reach**. The 0xC400_0000 aperture answers SEP_IN AXI reads but takes no writes from it -- Known Limitations records that measurement. The CPU is the only master that can program it.
* The loop `smc_ext_interrupts_pin_test` states in its own docstring that it leaves open: that testcase follows `ext_interrupts_i[0]` through `prim_sync3` and "does not claim PLIC". `smc_hang_detector_plic_route_test` checks a route with testbench probes and reads no PLIC register either. This closes it end to end: pin -> synchroniser -> gateway -> enable and priority against threshold -> MEIP -> trap -> claim -> the ID the CPU reads back -> complete.
* PLIC source 1, which is `cpu_interrupts[0]` and therefore `ext_interrupts_i[0]`: the SMC interrupt-vector map (`doc/interrupts.adoc`, "SMC CPU Interrupt Vector Map") places the external interrupts at the bottom of the vector and defines the PLIC source ID as the bit index plus one because ID 0 is reserved; `tt_smc_interrupts.h` transcribes that chapter. It is source 1 and not another because `tb_top.sv:1199-1200` drives only `ext_interrupts_i[1:0]` -- bit 0 from `tb_ext_interrupt_0_i`, bit 1 from `tb_temp_interrupt_i` -- and ties `[255:2]` to zero, and this testcase raises bit 0 alone: any other external source is a line nothing raises here, so the handler would never run and the testcase would time out instead of measuring anything.
* No forced internal state: the stimulus is a DUT input pin and everything between it and the verdict is the DUT ([NO-FORCED-INTERNAL-STATE]).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Build `plic_sanity` through `[c_build.default]`, stage `plic_sanity.ecc.hex`, hold `boot_stall` from t=0, clear CPU_CTRL SCRATCH_0 and read the clear back.
. Program the reset vector and release. The image clears every context's enable words, gives the eight contexts distinct thresholds, registers a handler for source 1, enables it, publishes the arm word 0xAAAA_AAAA and parks in `wfi`. The handler masks the source through the context threshold (not the enable bit, which the PLIC requires set for a completion to count) and returns, so the driver's claim-complete write executes; main then lowers the threshold with the pin still high and requires a second delivery before it posts the verdict, because the gateway forwards a source again only after its previous claim was completed.
. Require the arm word before any verdict. A PASS not preceded by it is failed: the image would have reached its verdict without passing through the state the stimulus acts on.
. Hold the pin low for 2000 further `clk_smc_i` cycles and require SCRATCH_0 to still read the arm word.
. Drive `ext_interrupts_i[0]` high and poll for the verdict.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-FW-PLIC-QUIET` |With the PLIC armed and the pin still low, SCRATCH_0 does not move. Without this the PASS could have come from any interrupt already pending, and the testcase would be satisfied by a DUT that traps on something else
|`CHK-FW-PLIC-STIMULUS` |The pin rises only after the quiet window, so the verdict below is ordered after the stimulus rather than merely coincident with it
|`CHK-FW-PLIC-CLAIM` |The firmware's own PASS word reaches CPU_CTRL SCRATCH_0, and only two completed claims of the registered source can put it there: the verdict is posted after the source, unmasked with the pin still high, is delivered a second time, which the PLIC gateway does only once the first claim has been completed. `riscv_plic0.c:106-107` dispatches `metal_exint_table[idx]` on the index the claim register returned, and the image registers a handler at one index only -- every other source reaches `__metal_plic0_default_handler`, which posts no verdict. The handler's own ID compare is unreachable for that reason and is not credited here
|===

*Pass Criteria*: all three checkers hold. The verdict poll is bounded at 6000 iterations of 100 `clk_smc_i` cycles; the image spends most of the boot in `__metal_driver_riscv_plic0_init`, which walks all 336 sources over MMIO. No `ci` tag: the hosted runners have no RISC-V toolchain. Enrolled in: `all`, `fw`.

'''''

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CSR-BOUNDARY-SWEEP` |First/last registers of the SCRATCH_COLD and SCRATCH_COLD_WARM windows and the first register of CHIP_CONFIG read their RDL value over SEP_IN AXI; the last CHIP_CONFIG register, LC_STATE (`sw = r; hw = w`, the lifecycle value the SEP drives), is derived from the window size, answers OKAY and is reported, not compared; the last register of each scratch window reads back its written pattern and then its saved value; all 16 accesses were checked by the scoreboard. Fails on a read mismatch, a non-OKAY response, a wrong access count, or traffic that never reached the scoreboard
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-CSR-BOUNDARY-SWEEP` holds after its compares and every sequence assert holds. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-009-smc_register_sanity_test]]
===== 9 `smc_register_sanity_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_register_sanity_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Run the SMC OSS register-sanity scenario.

*Test Coverage*:

* read reset value from SMC scratch registers,
* write distinct patterns,
* read back the exact values.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_register_sanity_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CSR-SCRATCH-RW-RESTORE` |Each of the 3 scratch CSRs reads its generated RDL reset, reads back its written pattern, and reads back the reset again after the restore write, over 15 SEP_IN AXI accesses (cocotb shape). Fails on any readback mismatch, a non-OKAY response, a missing catalog reset constant, or a wrong access count
|`CHK-CSR-IDLE-VALUE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): idle read of each scratch register is zero after warm-reset release
|`CHK-CSR-RANDOM-READBACK` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): each seeded random pattern reads back on the same register
|`CHK-CSR-READBACK` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): directed write pattern reads back on the same register
|`CHK-CSR-RESTORE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): restore write of zero reads back
|`CHK-CSR-RESP` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): every SEP_IN CSR access completes with an OKAY response
|`CHK-FUSE-SENSE-DONE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): warm-reset domain release after fuse sense completes
|`CHK-NONVAC` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`; no run in this package executes that framework, so this row is not closed by any run here): non-vacuity count of real SEP_IN CSR accesses in `smc_register_sanity_test_seq.svh`
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-CSR-SCRATCH-RW-RESTORE` holds after its compares: every SEP_IN read carries the value it must return (RDL reset, written pattern, restored reset; five accesses per register), the scoreboard fails the run on a mismatch, and the sequence asserts the access count against the register list. The seven `SV-UVM shape only` rows are the UVM shape's checkers; no run in this package executes `--framework uvm` (every `result.json` under `build/runs/` records `framework: cocotb`), so they close nothing here and are listed for that shape only. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `smoke`.
'''''

==== POWER

[[sec-010-smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test]]
===== 10 `smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: AVS_CFG answers with AVS_CG_EN cleared and stops answering when set.

*Test Coverage*:

* **Positive control** -- with the gate CLEARED, all three AVS_CFG windows must COMPLETE, and their real round-trip latency is *measured* here rather than assumed ([NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]).
* **Negative leg** -- with the gate SET, the same three reads must fail to answer within a bound DERIVED from that measurement (`_GATED_BOUND_FACTOR` x the slowest healthy round-trip, floored at `_MIN_GATED_BOUND_NS`). A fixed bound shorter than a healthy CSR round-trip would hold on a fully awake AVSBus -- it would measure latency, not decode ([NO-ALWAYS-PASS-CHECKER]) -- which is why the bound is measured, and why the same-bound ungated control below shows a responsive window satisfies it.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-AVSBUS-CG` |Named compare in `smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-SIDEBAND-OBSERVABILITY` |Named compare in `smc_sideband_vip_utils.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-AVSBUS-CG`, `CHK-SIDEBAND-OBSERVABILITY` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-013-smc_dma_sanity_test]]
===== 13 `smc_dma_sanity_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_dma_sanity_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), `SmcJtagAxiAgent` (JTAG-AXI bench agent over the `tb_top` JTAG-AXI bridge)

*Objective*: Run DMA copy over output-fabric payload bytes and check the model.

*Test Coverage*:

* DMA source-to-destination payload check through the output-fabric responder.
* A second descriptor with LENGTH = 1 separates "the programmed length was honoured" from "a whole 64-bit beat was copied": the destination word's upper seven bytes must still read the poison this leg wrote.
* The SYS_OUT responder is shared, and the pass-all outbound filter this sequence programs lets other masters' traffic complete on it, so each leg's traffic is counted per address from the address-channel handshake of its own transaction rather than from the responder's global transaction counters.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_dma_sanity_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DMA-OUTPUT-FABRIC-TRAFFIC` |Per leg and per address: exactly one read transaction at the source, one write transaction at the destination and one verification read at the destination reached the SYS_OUT responder. A transaction sent to the wrong address, or a second one at the same address, fails. The number of transactions other masters completed on the same responder is printed on the same line as reported, not asserted
|===


*Pass Criteria*: both copies match their expected destination words, the LENGTH = 1 leg leaves the destination's upper seven bytes at the poison, and each leg addressed the SYS_OUT responder exactly once per direction. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

Fails on a wrong MODE, RUNNING not set, COUNT outside the bound, missing pad edges, a COUNT that does not drop on TIMER_START, or one that does not advance

'''''

[[sec-017-smc_telemetry_receiver_csr_test]]
===== 17 `smc_telemetry_receiver_csr_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_telemetry_receiver_csr_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: U4-6: TELEMETRY CSR reset + INTR_TEST -> tb_telemetry_irq_any.

*Test Coverage*:

* The ATB framing the sequence drives is a DV-owned table. 8-bit beats assembled LSB-first into 64-bit packets, 9-bit blocks, the 5-bit probe ID and the last-packet flag are cited to `hw/ip/telemetry_receiver/doc/interface.adoc`, `doc/architecture.adoc` and `regs/telemetry_receiver.rdl` (`TELEMETRY_PROBE_ID.PROBE_ID[4:0]`, `INTR_STATUS.MISSING_LAST`).
* The bit positions of the probe-ID header, the last-packet flag and the first block of each packet are fixed by no document and are declared as DV-owned framing assumptions in the sequence.
* What is scored is the CSR-visible consequence of a message framed per that table (STATUS.EMPTY clearing, PROBE_ID reading back the framed value), not that the table is the specified telemetry format.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_telemetry_receiver_csr_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-TELEMETRY-RECEIVER-CSR` |Named compare in `smc_telemetry_receiver_csr_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-TELEMETRY-RECEIVER-<rx>-ATB` |Per receiver: after its ATB message STATUS leaves EMPTY within a bounded poll and TELEMETRY_PROBE_ID reads back the probe ID framed into that receiver, in `smc_telemetry_receiver_csr_test_seq.py`; expiry or a mismatch fails the run
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-TELEMETRY-RECEIVER-CSR` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-019-smc_wdt_timeout_pin_test]]
===== 19 `smc_wdt_timeout_pin_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_wdt_timeout_pin_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI); `tb_top.sv` sticky latches `tb_wdt_first_timeout_seen` / `tb_wdt_second_timeout_seen` on the `smc_wrapper` timeout outputs

*Objective*: A programmed first timeout raises `smc_wdt_first_timeout_o` at the DUT boundary; left uncleared, the stage-2 count in `CPU_CTRL.WDT_TIMEOUT` raises `smc_wdt_second_timeout_o`, and the reset unit turns that into the warm reset.

*Test Coverage*:

* core-0 WDT armed over SEP_IN with the magic-key protocol of `wdt.rdl` (`KEY` before each of `CMP`, `COUNT`, `CTRL`; `CTRL.wdogrsten | wdogenalways`);
* `smc_wdt_first_timeout_o` latched within a bound of arming, with `smc_wdt_second_timeout_o` still 0 on that cycle;
* `smc_wdt_second_timeout_o` latched `WDT_TIMEOUT` (`cpu_ctrl.rdl` reset `0x4000`) cycles after the first, within a stated slack;
* `rst_warm` still released on the cycle the first timeout latches, asserted after the second timeout, then released; both live pins read 0 afterwards while both sticky latches still read 1.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_wdt_timeout_pin_test_seq`. Each checker below is a bounded observation of a `tb_top` pin the DUT can fail to drive; a token is emitted only after that observation holds. `+smc_wdt_timeout_negative` inverts the second-timeout expectation (demands it on the cycle the first one latches) and must FAIL; it is the control for the second-timeout observer.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-FIRST` |`tb_wdt_first_timeout_seen` reaches 1 within `FIRST_TIMEOUT_BOUND_CYCLES` of the arming readback, `tb_wdt_second_timeout_seen` still 0 on that cycle
|`CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-SECOND` |`tb_wdt_second_timeout_seen` reaches 1 between `WDT_TIMEOUT` and `WDT_TIMEOUT + SECOND_TIMEOUT_SLACK_CYCLES` cycles after the first
|`CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-RESET` |`tb_rst_warm_smc_clk_n` reads 1 on the cycle the first timeout latches, falls within `WARM_RESET_BOUND_CYCLES` of the second timeout and rises again; live pins then 0, sticky latches 1
|===

*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-FIRST`, `CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-SECOND`, `CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-RESET` hold after their observations. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-020-smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test]]
===== 20 `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), `SmcJtagAxiAgent` (JTAG-AXI bench agent over the `tb_top` JTAG-AXI bridge)

*Objective*: Run zeroer over output-fabric payload bytes and check the model.

*Test Coverage*:

* Zeroer datapath payload check through the output-fabric responder.
* CTRL_STATUS is read back after the payload is proven cleared and compared exactly with STATUS masked: INT_EN latched, reserved bits zero (`CHK-ZEROER-CTRL-STATUS`). No level is asserted for STATUS itself, because the RDL describes the field as "completed" while the implemented field is observed to read the opposite way round (open issue #1234); the resting level is recorded instead.
* A second, longer zeroing is run purely as the positive control for STATUS: the field must leave its resting level while that zeroing is in flight (`CHK-ZEROER-STATUS-BUSY-ASSERTED`) and return to it once the zeroing completes (`CHK-ZEROER-STATUS-LIFECYCLE`), so a tied or unconnected bit fails without the test taking a side on which level means busy.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-NONVAC` |Non-vacuity count in `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-ZEROER-CMD-READBACK` |DEST_ADDR and SIZE read back the values the trigger step wrote, in `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test_seq.py`; the assert fails the run on a mismatch
|`CHK-ZEROER-CTRL-STATUS` |CTRL_STATUS with STATUS masked equals the packed INT_EN=1 word exactly, in `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test_seq.py`; STATUS's resting level is recorded, not asserted (#1234)
|`CHK-ZEROER-REGION-DECODE` |Named compare in `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-ZEROER-REGION-ZEROED` |Named compare in `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-ZEROER-STATUS-BUSY-ASSERTED` |STATUS read away from its resting level within a bounded poll while the long zeroing is in flight; expiry fails
|`CHK-ZEROER-STATUS-LIFECYCLE` |STATUS back at its resting level within a bounded poll after that zeroing retires, and the rest of the word again equals the INT_EN=1 word exactly; expiry or a mismatch fails
|`CHK-ZEROER-TRIGGER-STARTS` |Named compare in `smc_zeroer_dma_timeout_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-NONVAC`, `CHK-ZEROER-CTRL-STATUS`, `CHK-ZEROER-REGION-DECODE`, `CHK-ZEROER-REGION-ZEROED`, `CHK-ZEROER-STATUS-BUSY-ASSERTED`, `CHK-ZEROER-STATUS-LIFECYCLE` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-184-smc_dma_reserved_stream_banks_test]]
===== 184 `smc_dma_reserved_stream_banks_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_dma_reserved_stream_banks_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (no external VIP); `tb_dma_busy` sampled on every `clk_smc_i` edge across the sweep

*Objective*: All sixteen DMA stream banks decode with `NUM_CTRL_STREAMS = 1`: the reserved `STATUS`, `DONE` and `NEXT_ID` registers complete OKAY and read the value `dma.adoc` gives a reserved bank, and no reserved `NEXT_ID` read launches a transfer.

*Test Coverage*:

* The zeros are given teeth: `DST_ADDRESS_LO` in the same block takes and returns a pattern before the sweep and is restored after it, so a bank reading 0 is reading from a block that answers rather than from a dead bus.
* `tb_dma_busy` is required resolvable and 0 at every sampled `clk_smc_i` edge across the whole sweep, and the watcher is required to have sampled at least one edge, so "no transfer started" is a per-sample compare and not an untaken branch.
* Bank 0 is the functional stream: its `STATUS_0` and `DONE_0` are hardware status the specification pins to no value, so they are read for an OKAY response only and their values are reported, not compared. `NEXT_ID_0` is not read here, because reading it launches a transfer.
* The DMA clock gate is saved, ungated for the sweep, and restored with an exact readback.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_dma_reserved_stream_banks_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer: ungate the DMA clock, start the busy watcher, write and read back the liveness pattern, read `STATUS_0` and `DONE_0` for a response, sweep `STATUS_1..15`, `DONE_1..15` and `NEXT_ID_1..15` against an exact 0, restore the pattern and the clock gate, then require the busy count to be 0 and the access count to have reached its floor. The leaf re-checks the scoreboard compare count and books a protocol-VIP record.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DMA-STREAM-BANKS` |The cell ledger: the `all-16-banks-decode` cell with the measured evidence that closed it -- the reserved sweep's exact zeros, bank 0's reported status, the liveness pattern and the sampled busy count. Entered only after those compares passed
|`CHK-DMA-RESERVED-STREAM-BANKS` |Summary line: banks decoded, SEP_IN accesses issued, and the busy-high count out of the sampled cycles. Logged after the busy compare and the stimulus floor have held
|`CHK-DMA-STREAM-BANKS-FLOOR` |The leaf requires the scoreboard's `sys_axi_value_checks_seen` to have reached `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` before this token is logged
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every reserved `STATUS`, `DONE` and `NEXT_ID` register reads exactly 0 over an OKAY response, the liveness pattern reads back exactly, `tb_dma_busy` is 0 at all sampled cycles, and the scoreboard books at least `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` compares. Bank 0's status values are reported only. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''


==== FABRIC

[[sec-028-smc_filter_multi_entry_test]]
===== 28 `smc_filter_multi_entry_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_filter_multi_entry_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Filter 16 inbound + 16 outbound CONFIG sweep.

*Test Coverage*:

* RTL exposes 16 inbound + 16 outbound filter entries; this sweep covers every one of the 32 slots rather than entry 0 of each direction.
* Each of the 32 slots is proven *individually discriminable*, not merely "a window that answers 0x3000": every slot gets a signature value unique to (direction, index), and the sweep is PHASED so that the 32 signatures are CO-RESIDENT when they are read back.
* Phasing is the whole point.
* A per-slot write/readback/restore cycle proves nothing about aliasing: only one slot is ever non-default at a time, so an RTL that mapped all 32 windows onto one physical register would return the value just written through the very same address and pass.
* Here phase 2 programs all 32 slots without restoring any of them, and phase 3 reads all 32 back while all 32 are still resident -- so a read of slot i can only return sig_i if slot i has storage of its own.
* Under total aliasing the register holds sig_31 when phase 3 starts and the very first readback fails; under any partial aliasing of two windows the later-written one overwrites the earlier and that slot's readback fails.
* Phase 1 checks the RDL reset content of every window before anything is written, and phases 4/5 restore and re-confirm the RDL default, so the run also carries reset-value evidence.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_filter_multi_entry_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-FILTER-MULTI-ENTRY-SLOT-IDENTITY` |Named compare in `smc_filter_multi_entry_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-FILTER-MULTI-ENTRY-SLOT-IDENTITY` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-029-smc_input_fabric_axi_wr_rd_test]]
===== 29 `smc_input_fabric_axi_wr_rd_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_input_fabric_axi_wr_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), ocah_jtag_vip

*Objective*: Run the input/output fabric CSR precheck over SEP_IN AXI.

*Test Coverage*:

* P0 leaf for the input/output fabric CSR precheck sequence.
* DV-CARD: SMC_004 ANCHOR: smc_input_fabric_axi_wr_rd_test This is not an alias of `smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test`.
* That sibling programs the filters and then issues JTAG-AXI traffic into the output-fabric window.
* This leaf is the only enrolled owner of `smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test_seq` (SEP_IN CSR precheck of the same filter/remap windows).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test_seq` over SEP_IN AXI. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ALIAS-REMAP-RESET-DEFAULT` |Named compare in `smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-NONVAC` |Non-vacuity count in `smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test_seq.py`
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-ALIAS-REMAP-RESET-DEFAULT`, `CHK-NONVAC` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-030-smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test]]
===== 30 `smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), `SmcJtagAxiAgent` (JTAG-AXI bench agent over the `tb_top` JTAG-AXI bridge)

*Objective*: Run output-fabric write/read through JTAG AXI with responder checks.

*Test Coverage*:

* Output-fabric filter programming over SEP_IN AXI (`output_fabric_pass_all_cfg_seq`), then one JTAG-AXI write and read through the output-fabric window checked against the SYS_OUT responder and the scoreboard's `SmcMemoryModel` golden.
* The alias-remap reset-default compare is not on this path: `smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test_seq` is driven by `smc_input_fabric_axi_wr_rd_test`, which declares `CHK-ALIAS-REMAP-RESET-DEFAULT`.
* The leaf files a protocol-VIP record with the measured SEP_IN CSR and JTAG-AXI access counts against literal floors (`smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test.py`); the scoreboard rejects the record, and the run fails, below either floor.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `output_fabric_pass_all_cfg_seq` over SEP_IN AXI, then a JTAG-AXI write and read at the alternate output-fabric address. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-INPUT-OUTPUT-FABRIC-WR-RD` |The JTAG-AXI write then read at the alternate output-fabric address complete OKAY, the SYS_OUT responder's `last_addr` / `last_wdata` match that address and data, its write and read counters each advanced by one, and the scoreboard's memory-model golden records at least one update and one check (`smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test.py`)
|`CHK-NONVAC` |The protocol-VIP record is accepted with the measured SEP_IN CSR access count against its literal floor and the measured JTAG-AXI access count against its own (`smc_input_output_fabric_wr_rd_test.py`)
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-INPUT-OUTPUT-FABRIC-WR-RD`, `CHK-NONVAC` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-032-smc_output_fabric_slverr_inject_test]]
===== 32 `smc_output_fabric_slverr_inject_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_output_fabric_slverr_inject_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), `SmcJtagAxiAgent` (JTAG-AXI bench agent over the `tb_top` JTAG-AXI bridge)

*Objective*: OKAY WR/RD, then the SYS_OUT responder's one-shot SLVERR and DECERR on both directions, each carried back to the manager unchanged, and an OKAY recovery on the same address.

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OSS U1-2/U1-3/U6-2: SYS_OUT error responses through the slave agent's one-shot faults.
* The outbound fabric is a conduit: an error the subordinate raises is the subordinate's verdict, and the fabric's obligation is to hand the requesting manager the same encoding, neither downgraded nor swallowed. Each injected code is observed on the SYS_OUT wire by the passive monitor and required of the manager, so a fabric that rewrote it fails.
* The response code is an exact expectation on each access, so a DECERR is not satisfied by a SLVERR.
* The errored accesses leave the stored word untouched, and the OKAY write/read pair that follows on the same address shows no error state was latched.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_output_fabric_slverr_inject_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SYS-OUT-ERROR-CODES` |Each injected response code -- read SLVERR, write SLVERR, write DECERR and read DECERR -- was counted exactly once on the SYS_OUT wire by the passive monitor and received by the JTAG-AXI manager with the same encoding, and the OKAY write/read pair that follows on the same address completed normally
|===


*Pass Criteria*: the OKAY path completes and is booked against the memory model, each injected error reaches the manager with the encoding it had on the SYS_OUT wire, the stored word is untouched by the errored accesses, and the following OKAY write/read pair completes normally. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-033-smc_output_fabric_wr_rd_responder_test]]

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-OUTPUT-FABRIC-WR-RD-GOLDEN` |A JTAG-AXI 64-bit write to 0x0200_0000 followed by a read through the output fabric advances the SYS_OUT responder write counter by exactly 1 and the read counter by at least 1, leaves last_addr/last_wdata equal to the write, and the read data matches the scoreboard memory-model golden (updates >= 1, checks >= 1). Fails on an extra or missing responder write beat, a missing read beat, a stale last_addr/last_wdata, an X/Z observability counter, a golden mismatch, or a read the scoreboard never checked
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-OUTPUT-FABRIC-WR-RD-GOLDEN` holds after its compares and every sequence assert holds. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''

[[sec-035-smc_remap_cla_test]]
===== 35 `smc_remap_cla_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_remap_cla_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), `SmcJtagAxiAgent` (JTAG-AXI bench agent over the `tb_top` JTAG-AXI bridge)

*Objective*: ALIAS_REMAP address translation, remap-table reset sweep, CLA window.

*Test Coverage*:

* **ALIAS_REMAP address translation** -- entry 0 is programmed (`REGION_START` / `REGION_END` / `REGION_ATTRS.offset|valid`) and a JTAG AXI write issued at the source address is observed **landing at the translated address** on the SYS_OUT responder (`tb_output_axi_last_addr`). The un-programmed identity case is the same-run positive control and the entry-disabled re-read is the same-run negative control, so "the remap moved the access" is distinguishable from "nothing happened".
* **SMC_MMODE_REMAP_0..7 / SMC_ALIAS_REMAP_0..7 / SMC_XVISOR_REMAP_0..7** -- the RDL reset content of every entry (`expected=` on every read, not merely an OKAY response), and per-entry decode: every register of each table holds a distinct pattern inside its writable field while the rest of the table holds theirs, reads it back exactly and is restored, so an aliased pair or an undecoded window answering OKAY with zeros fails the readback. The reset reads alone cannot make that distinction, since every remap register resets to zero.
* **SMC_CLA_REG** -- the Cluster Local Aggregator window: an allow leg on real CLA registers (RDL reset values + a scratch write/readback) paired with the in-window-hole leg. Unallocated offsets inside the map complete OKAY with data 0; the five non-zero reset rows and the scratch write/readback distinguish a live aperture from a hole.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_remap_cla_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CLA-RESET-SWEEP` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-CLA-WINDOW-ALLOW` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-CLA-WINDOW-DENY` |Unmapped in-window CLA offsets complete OKAY with data 0 (`smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`); the run fails if a hole returns an error or non-zero data
|`CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-IDENTITY` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-LANDING` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-OFF` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-REMAP-TABLE-CORESIDENT` |The 8 XVISOR ATTRS, 8 MMODE ATTRS and 24 ALIAS START/END/ATTRS registers each held a distinct pattern written before any of its table was read back, read it back exactly and read back the RDL reset after the restore write, every readback a scoreboard value compare (`smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`); an aliased pair or an OKAY-zero hole fails the first readback of the pair
|`CHK-REMAP-TABLE-RESET` |All 40 MMODE/ALIAS/XVISOR remap registers answer OKAY and match their generated RDL reset value in a scoreboard value compare (`smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`)
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-CLA-RESET-SWEEP`, `CHK-CLA-WINDOW-ALLOW`, `CHK-CLA-WINDOW-DENY`, `CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-IDENTITY`, `CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-LANDING`, `CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-OFF`, `CHK-REMAP-TABLE-CORESIDENT`, `CHK-REMAP-TABLE-RESET` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.
'''''
